if self.project_id != self.parent_contract.project_id:\\n 276→            raise ValueError(\\\"计划项目身份与父合同不一致\\\")\\n 277→        promoted_ids = [item.source_version_id for item in self.promoted]\\n 278→        if len(set(promoted_ids)) != len(promoted_ids):\\n 279→            raise ValueError(\\\"晋级候选不得重复\\\")\\n 280→        affected_pairs = [(item.object_kind, item.object_id) for item in self.affected_objects]\\n 281→        reuse_pairs = [(item.object_kind, item.object_id) for item in self.reuse_objects]\\n 282→        if len(set(affected_pairs)) != len(affected_pairs):\\n 283→            raise ValueError(\\\"影响对象集合不得重复\\\")\\n 284→        if len(set(reuse_pairs)) != len(reuse_pairs):\\n 285→            raise ValueError(\\\"复用对象集合不得重复\\\")\\n 286→        if set(affected_pairs) & (\\\"claim\\\", \\\"page\\\", \\\"format\\\") for item in self.affected_objects\\n 293→        ):\\n 294→            # 影响至少要能落到事实、声明、页面和格式层级（由影响闭包传播）；\\n 295→            # 只停留在来源层级说明依赖边缺失，失败关闭。\\n 296→            raise ValueError(\\\"影响集合只停留在来源层级：缺少事实/声明/页面/格式依赖边\\\")\\n 297→        if self.affected_report_kinds and self.gate_override is None:\\n 298→            raise 301→        if self.promoted and not self.rebuild_report_kinds:\\n 302→            raise ValueError(\\\"晋级候选必须通过影响闭包定位到至少一类报告\\\")\\n 303→        if set(self.rebuild_report_kinds) & set(self.reuse_report_kinds):\\n 304→            raise ValueError(\\\"同一报告不能同时受影响又按摘要复用\\\")\\n 305→        if self.gate_override is not None and not self.affected_report_kinds:\\n 306→            raise ValueError(\\\"门槛收紧必须声明受影响报告集合\\\")\\n 307→        if self.gate_override is not None and \\\"gate_tightening\\\" not in self.change_kinds:\\n 308→            raise self.parent_contract.data_cutoff\\n 312→        ):\\n 313→            raise ValueError(\\\"截止日扩展必须实际扩大数据截止日\\\")\\n 314→        expected_digest = _payload_digest(self._digest_body())\\n 315→        if \\\"\\\"\\\"从最近已接受版本继续的增量刷新服务：plan / record / accept / resume。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 470→\\n 471→    def __init__(self, project_root: Path) -> None:\\n 472→        self.project_root = 476→        except MigrationError as error:\\n 477→            raise RefreshServiceError(f\\\"项目数据库迁移无法执行：{error}\\\") from error\\n 478→        self.event_store = EventStore(self.project_root)\\n 479→        self.snapshot_store = SnapshotStore(self.project_root)\\n 480→\\n 481→    # ── 读取与恢复视图 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 482→\\n 483→    def _load_project_document(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n 484→        path = self.project_root / \\\"project.yaml\\\"\\n 485→        try:\\n 486→            document = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\"))\\n 487→        except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError) as error:\\n 488→            raise RefreshStateError(\\\"项目文件缺失或不是有效 \\\"\\\"\\\"最近已接受的合同版本；项目文件中的历史版本保持只读。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 495→        document = self._load_project_document()\\n 496→        versions = document.get(\\\"project_contract_versions\\\")\\n 497→        if not isinstance(versions, list) or not versions:\\n 498→            raise RefreshStateError(\\\"项目文件没有可读取的合同版本\\\")\\n 499→        active = document[\\\"active_contract_version\\\"]\\n 500→        for payload in versions:\\n 501→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):\\n 502→                continue\\n 503→            if payload.get(\\\"contract_version\\\") != active:\\n 504→                continue\\n 505→            try:\\n 506→                return ProjectContract.model_validate(payload)\\n 507→            except ValueError as error:\\n 508→                raise        \\\"\\\"\\\"从规范事件流重建刷新计划；事件是真源，可安全跨中断调用。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 513→        for event in self.event_store.read_all():\\n 514→            if event.event_type != _PLAN_EVENT or event.payload.get(\\\"refresh_id\\\") != refresh_id:\\n 515→                continue\\n 516→            plan = RefreshPlan.model_validate(event.payload[\\\"plan\\\"])\\n 517→            if RefreshStateError(f\\\"刷新计划不存在：{refresh_id}\\\")\\n 521→\\n 522→    def resume_status(self, refresh_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n 523→        \\\"\\\"\\\"中断恢复视图：计划、已记录动作、缺口与接受状态。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 524→        plan = self.load_plan(refresh_id)\\n 525→        actions = self._rebuild_actions(refresh_id)\\n 526→        decisions = self._gate_decisions(refresh_id)\\n 527→        snapshots = self._registered_snapshot_ids(refresh_id)\\n 528→        qc_records = self._qc_records(refresh_id)\\n 529→        required = {(item.object_kind, item.object_id) for item in plan.affected_objects}\\n 530→        recorded = {(item.object_kind, item.object_id) for item in actions}\\n 531→        return {\\n 532→            \\\"plan\\\": plan,\\n 533→            \\\"recorded_actions\\\": actions,\\n 534→            \\\"gate_decisions\\\": decisions,\\n 535→            \\\"registered_report_snapshot_ids\\\": snapshots,\\n 536→            \\\"scientific_qc_records\\\": qc_records,\\n 537→ 540→                for kind, object_id in sorted(required - recorded)\\n 541→            ),\\n 542→            \\\"pending_gate_report_kinds\\\": tuple(\\n 543→                sorted(\\n 544→ }\\n 561→\\n 562→    # ── 幂等账本 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 563→\\n 564→    def _claim(self, *, key: str, operation: str, result: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\\n 565→        \\\"\\\"\\\"登记操作结果；返回 True 表示首次登记，False 表示精确重放。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 566→        result_digest = _payload_digest(result)\\n 567→        with open_database(self.database_path) as database:\\n 568→            row = database.execute(\\n 569→                \\\"SELECT operation, result_digest FROM idempotency_keys WHERE idempotency_key = ?\\\",\\n 570→                (key,),\\n 571→            ).fetchone()\\n 572→            if row is not None:\\n 573→                if str(row[0]) != operation or str(row[1]) != result_digest:\\n 574→                    raise RefreshConflictError(f\\\"同一幂等键对应了不同刷新输入：{key}\\\")\\n 575→                return False\\n 576→            database.execute(\\n 577→                \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 578→                INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (\\n 579→                    idempotency_key, operation, result_digest, created_at\\n 580→                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\\n 581→                \\\"\\\"\\\",\\n 582→                (key, operation, result_digest, datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat()),\\n 583→            )\\n 584→        return True\\n 585→\\n 586→    def _recorded_digest(self, key: str) -> str | None:\\n 587→        with open_database(self.database_path) as database:\\n 588→            row = database.execute(\\n 589→                \\\"SELECT result_digest FROM idempotency_keys WHERE idempotency_key = ?\\\",\\n 590→                (key,),\\n 591→            ).fetchone()\\n 592→        return None if row is None else str(row[0])\\n 593→\\n 594→    # ── 事件 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 595→\\n 596→    def _append_event(\\n 597→        self,\\n 598→        *,\\n 599→        event_type: str,\\n 600→        event_kind:     project_id=project_id,\\n 617→            run_id=_RUN_ID,\\n 618→            event_type=event_type,\\n 619→            occurred_at=occurred_at,\\n 620→            actor_id=actor_id,\\n 621→            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,\\n 622→            payload=payload,\\n 623→        )\\n 624→        self.event_store.append(event)\\n 625→\\n 626→    # ── 操作 1：plan_refresh ───────────────────────────────────────────────\\n 627→\\n 628→    def plan_refresh(\\n 629→        self,\\n 630→        *,\\n 631→        requested_cutoff: str | datetime | None = None,\\n 632→ 输入失败关闭。重大合同变化不做局部刷新，明确要求重新建立基线。\\n 647→        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 648→        occurred_at = _offset_datetime(requested_at)\\n 649→        parent = self.parent_contract()\\n 650→\\n 651→        # 1. 重大合同变化分类：基线身份三维任一变化必须再基线（图定义分支）。\\n 652→        self._assert_incremental_scope(\\n 653→            parent,\\n 654→            evidence_contract_version=evidence_contract_version,\\n 655→    parent=parent,\\n 678→            child=child,\\n 679→            gate_specs=gate_specs,\\n 680→            declared_affected_report_kinds=declared_affected_report_kinds,\\n 681→            occurred_at=occurred_at,\\n 682→        )\\n 683→\\n 684→        change_kinds: list[str] = []\\n 685→        if cutoff_expansion:\\n 686→            change_kinds.append(\\\"cutoff_expansion\\\")\\n RefreshStateError(\\\"刷新请求没有可执行的增量变化：截止日未扩大，门槛也未收紧\\\")\\n 691→\\n 692→        # 5. 影响闭包：截止日扩展以晋级候选为种子；门槛收紧以受影响报告页面\\n 693→        #    为种子；传播与复用集合全部由影响图推导，本服务不复制传播规则。\\n 694→        (\\n 695→            affected_objects,\\n 696→            reuse_objects,\\n 697→            impact_report_kinds,\\n 698→            impact_plan_digest,\\n 699→        ) = self._impact_sets(\\n 700→            promoted_seed_ids=[item.source_version_id for item in promoted],\\n 701→            affected_report_kinds=affected_kinds,\\n 702→            impact_edges=impact_edges,\\n 703→ _append_receipt(self, action: RebuildAction) -> None:\\n1235→        \\\"\\\"\\\"追加重建回执 JSONL（投影）；事件流是真源，缺失行可由事件重放补齐。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n1236→        path = self._receipt_path()\\n1237→        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n1238→        existing_ids: set[str] = set()\\n1239→        if path.is_file():\\n1240→            for line in path.read_text(encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\").splitlines():\\n1241→                if line.strip():\\n1242→                    existing_ids.add(str(json.loads(line).get(\\\"action_id\\\")))\\n1243→        if action.action_id in existing_ids:\\n1244→            return\\n1245→        encoded = _canonical_json_text(action.model_dump(mode=\\\"json\\\")) + \\\"\\\\n\\\"\\n1246→        descriptor = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_APPEND | os.O_CREAT)\\n1247→        try:\\n1248→            os.write(descriptor, encoded.encode(\\\"utf-8\\\"))\\n1249→            os.fsync(descriptor)\\n1250→        finally:\\n1251→            os.close(descriptor)\\n1252→\\n1253→    def _rebuild_actions(self, refresh_id: str) -> tuple[RebuildAction, ...]:\\n1254→        \\\"\\\"\\\"从事件流重建全部重建回执，并自愈缺失的 JSONL 投影行。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n1255→        actions: dict[str, RebuildAction] = {}\\n1256→        for event in self.event_store.read_all():\\n1257→            if event.event_type != _REBUILD_EVENT:\\n1258→          raise RefreshConflictError(\\\"同一重建动作标识对应了不同内容\\\")\\n1265→            actions[action.action_id] = action\\n1266→        ordered = tuple(sorted(actions.values(), key=lambda item: item.action_id))\\n1267→        recorded_ids: set[str] = set()\\n1268→        path = self._receipt_path()\\n1269→        if path.is_file():\\n1270→            for line in 422→_ALLOWED_PARENT_EDGES: frozenset[tuple[GateObjectType, GateObjectType]] = frozenset(\\n 423→    {\\n 424→        (GateObjectType.PRODUCT, GateObjectType.TRIAL),\\n 425→ (GateObjectType.TIMEPOINT, snapshot.timepoint_ids),\\n1218→    )\\n1219→    proofs_by_type: dict[GateObjectType, EmptySetProof] = {}\\n1220→    for proof in snapshot.empty_set_proofs:\\n1221→ _assert_trial_design_closed(snapshot: ApplicableUniverseSnapshot) -> None:\\n1268→    \\\"\\\"\\\"每试验设计类型与关系图一致，且每个比较关联至少两个同试验组别。\\n1269→\\n1270→    比较设计必须有至少一条 trial→comparison 边；单臂设计不得有比较边。\\n1271→    每个比较必须关联至少两个不同组别，且全部组别位于比较所属试验内。\\n1272→    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n1273→    design_by_trial = {\\n1274→        record.trial_id: record for record in snapshot.trial_design_evidence\\n1275→    }\\n1276→ {}\\n1280→    for edge in snapshot.relationship_edges:\\n1281→        if edge.parent_type is GateObjectType.TRIAL:\\n1282→            if edge.child_type is GateObjectType.COMPARISON:\\n1283→                comparison_edges_by_trial[edge.parent_id] = (\\n1284→                    comparison_edges_by_trial.get(edge.parent_id, 0) + 1\\n1285→                )\\n1286→                comparison_trial[edge.child_id] = edge.parent_id\\n1287→            elif edge.child_type is GateObjectType.GROUP:\\n1288→                group_trial[edge.child_id] = edge.parent_id\\n1289→        elif comparison_count = comparison_edges_by_trial.get(trial_id, 0)\\n1298→        if record.design_kind is TrialDesignKind.COMPARATIVE:\\n1299→            if comparison_count < 1:\\n1300→ _assert_relationship_graph_closed(\\n1326→    snapshot: ApplicableUniverseSnapshot,\\n1327→    seen_ids: dict[str, GateObjectType],\\n1328→    collections: tuple[tuple[GateObjectType, tuple[str, ...]], ...],\\n1329→) -> None:\\n1330→    \\\"\\\"\\\"校验关系图：未知/重复边与歧义层级父边失败关闭，非产品对象必有规定父边。\\n1331→\\n1332→    层级父关系（product→trial、trial→comparison/group/endpoint、endpoint→timepoint）\\n1333→ | None = None,\\n1605→) -> GateUnitResult:\\n1606→    \\\"\\\"\\\"对单个对象的单个单元做确定性决定；适用性未确定时失败关闭。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n1607→    if not applicable and unit.applicability_predicate_id == binding.fact_version_id for binding in qualifying\\n1635→    )\\n1636→    satisfied_count = len(distinct_fact_version_ids)\\n1637→    if satisfied_count >= unit.threshold:\\n1638→        outcome = 725→## 17. 修订、刷新与可选监测\\n 726→\\n 727→### 17.1 来源关联修订\\n 728→\\n 729→HTML 中的事实、图点、矩阵单元和声明可以发起修订建议，但不能直接编辑科学真源。建议包含目标稳定 ID、当前报告/快照、可见值、建议值、理由和新来源。\\n 730→\\n 731→修订建议 schema 至少保存建议 ID、提交时间、目标事实/声明/图点 ID、报告与快照、旧值、建议值/解释、用户理由、附件/链接、验证状态、证据、冲突、影响集、批准者/决定和发布幂等键。\\n 732→\\n 733→宿主提供本地项目桥时，建议直接进入项目修订收件箱；纯 `file://` 报告必须能够导出自包含修订包供 Agent 摄取，不能依赖中心服务。\\n 734→\\n 735→Agent 核验来源身份、上下文和定位，生成旧值—新值、证据和下游影响范围。有效修订进入 `validated_pending_user_approval`；只有用户明确批准后才能建立新证据/声明快照并局部重建。拒绝或证据不足均保留追加式记录，不改变现行版本。\\n 736→\\n 737→### 17.2 增量刷新\\n 738→\\n 739→刷新从最近已接受快照、来源回执和缺口账本开始，对开发/招募/结果/监管/试验变更/论文/会议/公司事件等可变事实重新核验，并进行缺口和变化驱动检索。每次接受的刷新建立新的不可变证据、声明和报告版本，记录新增、变化、撤回、修订、取代和未变化对象。\\n 740→\\n 741→只重算受影响声明、视图和产物；未受影响对象按摘要复用。重大 schema、本体或证据合同变化触发显式再基线和迁移，历史版本保持可读。\\n 742→\\n 743→### 17.3 可选监测\\n 744→\\n 745→支持调度的宿主可以安装独立监测 Skill。监测只写入去重后的变更候选，不能自动接纳事实、生成正式快照或发布报告。用户决定是否启动正常刷新。移除监测能力不影响新建、打开、恢复或手动刷新项目。\\n 746→\\n 747→变更候选 schema 至少保存候选 ID、来源/版本/定位、发现时间、涉及实体和声明域、变化类型、当前值与候选值、去重摘要、可能影响的报告/页面、获取/诊断状态和用户处置；候选不能直接成为事实。\\n 748→\\n 749→## 18. Agent 交互、日志、安全与隐私\\n 750→\\n 751→### 18.1 无强制工作台\\n 752→\\n 753→Codex、Hermes、OMP 等宿主的原生任务/对话就是运行交互面。基线包不包含强制 Web 工作台、服务器或中心仪表盘。可选观察器可以读取持久日志做状态展示，但不能持有科学真源。\\n 754→\\n 755→### 18.2 持久日志\\n 756→\\n 757→项目至少保存简洁 `status.md`、每次运行摘要、`events.jsonl`、来源回执、A/B/C 覆盖记录、下载请求、必要诊断和产物清单。日志足以支持 Agent 重启、上下文压缩、跨宿主交接和审计，不依赖模型记忆。\\n 758→\\n 759→### 18.3 凭据和用户登录\\n 760→\\n 761→用户可以在自己的浏览器完成登录、机构访问、验证码或同意。系统不得请求、复制、记录或打包密码、Cookie、会话令牌和授权头。用户辅助获取改变访问来源，不改变科学权威。\\n 762→\\n 763→手工文件先进入隔离目录，按摘要、嵌入元数据、标题页和试验/论文标识符匹配。重复、下载不完整、登录跳转页、HTML 错误页和身份不清文件不能进入证据库。\\n 764→\\n 765→\"}],\"is_error\":false}],\"role\":\"user\"},\"parent_tool_use_id\":null,\"_meta\":{\"traceparent\":\"00-a27c53152678ab5365c107c35a36b433-051d83662d8f4ecd-01\",\"baggage\":\"codebuddy.session_id=bb64d8b3-e202-4668-b8e6-a75b3c768e7a,codebuddy.conversation_request_id=a27c53152678ab5365c107c35a36b433\"},\"__timestamp\":\"2026-08-31T18:20:25.693Z\",\"_requestId\":\"a27c53152678ab5365c107c35a36b433\"}\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"01a0590d-30c4-7991-a090-775be1d579ce\",\"session_id\":\"bb64d8b3-e202-4668-b8e6-a75b3c768e7a\",\"message\":{\"id\":\"01a0590d-30c4-7991-a090-775be1d579ce\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"thinking\",\"thinking\":\"The §17.2 contract is short: refresh from latest accepted snapshot, new immutable evidence/claim/report versions, record new/changed/withdrawn/revised/superseded/unchanged objects, only recompute affected, major schema/ontology/evidence-contract change → explicit rebaseline + migration, history stays readable.\\n\\nThe implementation satisfies most of this. The \\\"记录新增、变化、撤回、修订、取代和未变化对象\\\" (record new/changed/withdrawn/revised/superseded/unchanged) — the refresh_node `refresh_reevaluate` declares `change_records` output (\\\"新增/变化/撤回/修订/取代记录标识\\\"), but the service doesn't actually implement change_records — the plan has `promoted` and `blocked` but no changed/withdrawn/revised/superseded records. The node contract declares it, but `RefreshService` never records change_records. That's a contract-vs-implementation gap: the graph contract says refresh_reevaluate outputs change_records and unchanged_digest, but the service plan has no such fields. The QC chain then binds the stale result's key, so the whole ledger is internally consistent but wrong. The existing test only catches this because it records the *correct* result first and then a conflicting one (`test_incremental_refresh.py:1196-1208`); if the parent-spec result were recorded first, it would pass. I statically traced all 10 integration-test scenarios against the implementation.\n\n**Verdict shape:** The implementation is well-constructed — deterministic impact closure, append-only receipts with event-replay self-healing, content-addressed snapshot locking, fail-closed acceptance ordering, Chinese user-facing messages, and HTML-only format enforcement are all genuinely present. The two-approval-node contract is coherent. However, I found **one high-impact defect and one high-medium defect that directly hit the review focus “真实门槛结果绑定”**, plus several medium gaps and a process-level evidence gap. The implementation should not be accepted until Codex decides on the two binding defects.\n\n### Finding 1 — HIGH: `record_gate_decision` does not bind the gate result to the child (tightened) spec fingerprint\n\nEvidence: `src/ci_workflow/application/refresh_service.py:1280-1322`. `record_gate_decision` validates `ReportGateResult` (which recomputes `result_key` and checks decision-consistency — good), then checks only (a) `report_kind in plan.rebuild_report_kinds` and (b) `contract_version == child version`. It **never checks `spec_fingerprint` against the child spec** and never checks `spec_version == gate_override.base_spec_version`.\n\nConsequence: for a gate-tightening refresh, a caller can evaluate with the **parent (looser) spec** at `contract_version=\"2\"`, record a PASSED result, and the refresh will be accepted — the tightening is silently bypassed. The QC chain then binds the stale result's key, so the whole ledger is internally consistent but wrong. The existing test only catches this because it records the *correct* result first and then a conflicting one (`test_incremental_refresh.py:1196-1208`); if the parent-spec result were recorded first, it would pass. This contradicts PRD 验收 3 (“GateSpec 只允许收紧；受影响报告回到覆盖/科学质控”) and the “真实门槛结果绑定” review focus.\n\nConcrete remediation:\n- Persist the per-kind child spec fingerprint in `RefreshPlan` (or in `GateOverride`), and in `record_gate_decision` verify `validated.spec_fingerprint == <child spec fingerprint for that kind>` and `validated.spec_version == override.base_spec_version`.\n- Add a regression test that records a parent-spec PASSED result **first** and asserts `RefreshStateError`.\n\n### Finding 2 — HIGH/MEDIUM: cutoff-expansion gate results are not bound to the accepted child's evidence snapshot\n\nEvidence: the test itself passes `evidence_snapshot_id=\"evidence-snapshot-refresh\"` into `_gate_result_for` (`test_incremental_refresh.py:990`), an ID that is never locked as a snapshot. `record_gate_decision` does not verify `evidence_snapshot_id` against any registered snapshot. At accept, `_lock_evidence_snapshot` (refresh_service.py:1713-1730) locks a *content-addressed* snapshot whose ID necessarily differs from the fabricated string, and nothing compares them.\n\nConsequence: the accepted child version is not provably the version that was gated; a gate result evaluated against a different evidence universe can be attached to the child. §17.2 requires the accepted refresh to establish new immutable evidence/claim/report versions with a coherent chain.\n\nConcrete remediation (pick one):\n- At accept for cutoff expansion, require `decision.gate_result.evidence_snapshot_id == <newly locked evidence snapshot id>`, or\n- Require the gate result's evidence snapshot to be a registered parent/planned snapshot at record time, and have the plan carry the intended new evidence snapshot identity.\n\n### Finding 3 — MEDIUM: “记录新增、变化、撤回、修订、取代和未变化对象” is not implemented\n\nEvidence: PRD 范围 (“仅重评新适格候选；记录新增、变化、撤回、修订、取代和未变化对象”) and §17.2 (“记录新增、变化、撤回、修订、取代和未变化对象”). The `refresh_reevaluate` node contract declares a `change_records` output (refresh.py:194), but `RefreshPlan` only carries `promoted` / `blocked` / `affected_objects` / `reuse_objects`. There is no changed/withdrawn/revised/superseded ledger anywhere in the service.\n\nRemediation options: implement a change-record field on the plan (and persist it), or explicitly document and get approval for a v1 scope cut. Either way the current state is a silent gap against the approved contract.\n\n### Finding 4 — MEDIUM: QC independence is self-declared; `review_input_digest` is unverified\n\nEvidence: refresh_service.py:1340-1404. Independence is enforced only by `reviewer_id != producer_id` (both caller-supplied strings). `review_input_digest` is format-validated but never tied to an actual review bundle; `source_refs`/`locators` in the verdict are not checked against real evidence. `candidate_content_digest` IS correctly bound to the registered report manifest and `gate_result_key` IS bound to the recorded decision — so the chain is partially solid.\n\nThis may be an acceptable v1 governance boundary (no auth exists in this system), but it must be stated explicitly; otherwise “独立科学质控” overstates what is proven.\n\n### Finding 5 — MEDIUM: impact graph is entirely caller-declared; format propagation not required\n\nEvidence: `plan_refresh` accepts `impact_nodes`/`impact_edges` as caller input and trusts them. The only guard is that the closure must reach claim/page/format. A sparse graph (omitted edges, omitted nodes) silently shrinks the affected set; there is no cross-check against registered project facts/claims/pages, and no rule that an affected page must propagate to the `html` format node. The “站点优先” scope (ADR 0013) is enforced only when a format object happens to be present in the closure.\n\n### Finding 6 — LOW/MEDIUM coverage gaps\n\n- Combined cutoff-expansion + gate-tightening path is untested (the code merges both seed sets; behavior is plausible but unverified).\n- Veto-QC path is untested (accept blocks on `verdict != \"accepted\"`, but no test drives it).\n- Page nodes with multiple `report_kinds` can over-expand `rebuild_report_kinds` to kinds the caller never intended to rebuild (conservative, but a trap).\n- Cosmetic: `refresh_reevaluate` declares `writes=(\"evidence\",)` with `side_effect_class=\"none\"`; `_tighten_gates` computes `reuse_kinds` that `_build_plan` immediately overwrites (dead code); refresh nodes are not wired into `graph/registry.py` / `definitions/__init__.py` (acceptable declarative-only v1 boundary, but should be stated).\n\n### Finding 7 — PROCESS/HIGH: execution evidence trail is empty\n\nEvidence: `metrics/ci-phase9-task92-incremental-refresh_execution_metrics.md` is all TODO; `reviews/codex_execution_..._review.md` is all TODO; worker reports `runs/execution/ci-phase9-task92-incremental-refresh/` do not exist; task.json is `in_progress` with `commit: null`; the only runtime trace is a `__pycache__` compiled pyc (proves collection/import under pytest 9.1.1, not GREEN). No logs show that pytest/Ruff/mypy were run. I could not execute tests in this session (Bash denied).\n\n## Evidence And Assumptions\n\n**Verified (observation):**\n- Four delivered files exist and are internally coherent: `src/ci_workflow/graph/impact.py`, `src/ci_workflow/graph/definitions/refresh.py`, `src/ci_workflow/application/refresh_service.py`, `tests/integration/test_incremental_refresh.py` (10 tests).\n- All schema dependencies exist in migrations (0002-0009): `source_versions`, `refresh_candidates`, `idempotency_keys`, `report_snapshots`, `content_blobs`, `source_date_assertions`;
